It’s as clear as if he’s standing right behind me when I'm out on the trail, or in my painting room. And even though part of me knows he’s dead... in the moment it happens, it feels completely real.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing Garrett's voice?\n\nPatient: Well, it started... I guess I only started hearing his voice during this really wired stretch I had recently. I was barely sleeping at all, taking on these impossible projects, and I just felt completely driven to keep going. \n\nI had never heard him like that before that mood shift. I mean, it wasn’t something that happened during ordinary grief. Um... it's like the things that usually anchor me just feel completely flat and unreachable now. It felt like a while, maybe a couple of weeks? I just remember it feeling like a really long stretch where I just couldn't stop moving or thinking, but I didn't exactly track the dates.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods of feeling sad or depressed since Garrett's death that lasted for two weeks or more?\n\nPatient: Yes, definitely longer than two weeks. The exact timeline is... it's a bit blurry for me. I don't honestly remember if the emptiness started before I got all wired like that, or if it was after. I just know I've been feeling this way for a while now, but I couldn't tell you exactly which came first.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel depressed before you started feeling wired and driven?\n\nPatient: Honestly, it's pretty much like I just said. The whole timeline is just a mess in my head right now. I really don't know if the depression started before I got into that wired phase, or if it came after. I just can't piece the exact order together.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel depressed before you started feeling wired and driven?\n\nPatient: Like I was saying, I really can't give you a straight answer on that. I'm just not sure which one happened first. The order of it all is just lost on me right now.\n\nDoctor: Did you have any depressive feelings before this wired, driven state started?\n\nPatient: I know you're trying to establish a clear timeline here, but asking it again really isn't going to change my answer. I just remember it feeling like a really long stretch where I just couldn't stop moving or thinking, but I didn't exactly track the dates.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods of feeling sad or depressed since Garrett's death that lasted for two weeks or more?\n\nPatient: Yes, definitely longer than two weeks. The depressed, shut-down part... it's been going on for about four months now.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel empty and like things had stopped meaning anything before this wired, driven state started?\n\nPatient: Um, I'm not really sure. The exact timeline is... it's a bit blurry for me. I don't honestly remember if the emptiness started before I got all wired like that, or if it was after. I just know I've been feeling this way for a while now, but I couldn't tell you exactly which came first.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel depressed before you started feeling wired and driven?\n\nPatient: Honestly, it's pretty much like I just said. The whole timeline is just a mess in my head right now. I really don't know if the depression started before I got into that wired phase, or if it came after. I mean, losing Garrett was... it was devastating, obviously. It's hard to separate it all out.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your depression is a direct response to Garrett's death?\n\nPatient: Like I just said, I'm really not sure. I mean... yes, his death was a huge blow, but I can't say if the depression is a direct response to just that. It's all mixed together right now, and I just don't have a clear answer for you.\n\nDoctor: Did you experience a period of feeling depressed before the period where you felt wired and driven?\n\nPatient: Well, like I mentioned a minute ago when you asked if it came after... I really just don't know. Before, after... the timeline is just completely gone for me right now.
